+    public static Dictionary<string,Term> AppearanceTokens(Term value)
+    {
+        value.Ground();var tokens=new Dictionary<string,Term>();
+        if(value.Kind=="atom"&&value.Name=="inherit")return tokens;
+        var appearance=value.Require("appearance",2);
+        if(appearance[0].Integer!=1)throw new FormatException("Unsupported GUI appearance version.");
+        foreach(var token in appearance[1].Items) {
+            var a=token.Require("token",2);string name=a[0].Name;
+            if(a[0].Kind!="atom"||!tokens.TryAdd(name,a[1]))throw new FormatException("Duplicate GUI appearance token.");
+            if(name is "panel" or "field" or "text" or "accent" or "edge" or "error" or "success") {
+                if(a[1].Require("rgb",3).Any(n=>n.Integer<0||n.Integer>255))throw new FormatException("Invalid GUI colour.");
+            } else if(name=="panel_opacity") {
+                if(a[1].Integer<0||a[1].Integer>1000)throw new FormatException("Invalid panel opacity.");
+            } else if(name is "spacing" or "padding" or "corner_radius") {
+                if(a[1].Integer<0||a[1].Integer>int.MaxValue)throw new FormatException("Unrenderable GUI appearance extent.");
+            } else throw new FormatException("Unsupported GUI appearance token: "+name);
+        }
+        return tokens;
+    }

+test('result delivery failure retains exact signed work and resolves without execution replay', async () => {
+  const previousFetch = globalThis.fetch, journal = memoryOperationJournal(), identity = signedIdentity()
+  const calls = []
+  globalThis.fetch = async (url, init) => {
+    calls.push({ url, body: JSON.parse(init.body) })
+    return url === '/api/goals/execute'
+      ? jsonResponse(503, { error: 'result_too_large' })
+      : jsonResponse(200, { result: 'operation_outcome', status: 'committed', terminal: true })
+  }
+  try {
+    await assert.rejects(signedGoal(identity, signedWrite(), { journal }),
+      error => error.status === 503 && error.outcomeUnknown === true)
+    const [original] = await journal.list()
+    assert.ok(original)
+    const [resolved] = await resolveSignedOperations(identity, { journal })
+    assert.equal(resolved.reply.status, 'committed')
+    assert.deepEqual(calls.map(x => x.url), ['/api/goals/execute', '/api/goals/outcomes'])
+    for (const field of ['request', 'signature']) {
+      assert.equal(calls[0].body[field], original[field])
+      assert.equal(calls[1].body[field], original[field])
+    }
+    assert.equal((await journal.list()).length, 0)
+  } finally { globalThis.fetch = previousFetch }
+})

-The seven resource values are allowances in the authorized descriptor. The
-rendering ontology bounds each allowance and the client measures actual GLB
-costs before Babylon sees the bytes. Transfer bytes include the complete GLB;
-geometry bytes count referenced vertex and index buffer views; image pixels
-sum embedded image dimensions. Vertex and triangle counts include each scene
-node that references a mesh, so repeated drawing consumes the allowance.
-Nodes and materials are checked separately. A value above its allowance
-produces a named error. The scaled visual span and reach from its pivot are
-also bounded by rendering ontology policy.
-These limits are ontology policy, not Erlang or JavaScript constants.
+The seven resource values are allowances in the authorized descriptor: a positive
+integer or `unlimited`. The vocabulary imposes no second global maximum. The
+client measures actual GLB costs before Babylon sees the bytes. Transfer bytes
+include the complete GLB; geometry bytes count referenced vertex and index buffer
+views; image pixels sum embedded image dimensions. Vertex and triangle counts
+include each scene node that references a mesh, so repeated drawing consumes an
+explicit allowance. Nodes and materials are checked separately. Exceeding a
+finite authored allowance produces a named error. Exact frame relationships and
+finite renderer conversion remain required; arbitrary extent and pivot-reach
+ceilings do not replace those checks.
+
+Godot's current ordinary scene adapter supports primitive box, wire-box, sphere
+and group descriptors. Its controller `tracked_model(...,asset(...))` is a
+separate existing profile with seven explicit finite allowances, checked by
+`ControllerMapping.Decode`, `StaticGlb.Admit` and the retained `RenderModels`
+owner. It does not consume the ordinary `mesh_asset` descriptor. This contract
+does not claim new native mesh or terrain presentation support.

+### Current authored appearance contract
+
+The shared container vocabulary admits `appearance` separately from semantic
+values and permissions. `inherit` uses its enclosing appearance or offline
+adapter defaults. `appearance(1, Tokens)` supplies unique `token(Name, Value)`
+entries: `panel`, `field`, `text`, `accent`, `edge`, `error` and `success` accept
+integer `rgb(R,G,B)` channels in 0–255; `panel_opacity` accepts integer permille
+0–1000; `spacing`, `padding` and `corner_radius` accept nonnegative logical units.
+An adapter refuses an extent it cannot represent. Unknown versions, names,
+duplicates and invalid values fail through the existing schema owner.
+
+`gui_compile/6` excludes only appearance from its semantic tree digest. Exact
+subject, declarations, bindings, continuation and preconditions remain covered.
+Native consumers retain the same controls and drafts while updating their
+existing theme resources. A signed refresh still withdraws interaction; retained
+presentation remains hidden until replacement authorization is accepted. Changes
+to hit geometry cancel an active capture. Denial and consumer replacement retire
+the retained presentation through the existing owners.
+
+Godot translates these tokens to native themes and style boxes. Foreground,
+controls, status, popups and the adjacent keyboard remain opaque; only authored
+panel backgrounds use alpha. Desktop opacity and the user's Opaque/Larger text
+preferences take precedence. The existing browser parser accepts `inherit` and
+explicitly rejects authored tokens until its renderer supports them; it does not
+silently substitute defaults. This adapter boundary does not certify physical
+headset readability or comfort.

 # Space and interaction architecture — implementation plan
 
+## Presentation limitations — accepted removal 2026-11-03
+
+**Shared-boundary implementation authorized:** after explicit disclosure, Yan said “I don’t want any hardcoded limit is that clear ?” and “I want this to be fixed now, I don’t count how many times you told me shit like this and now this is the nth time we are meeting this problem. And I don’t want shtty patches”. The exact original quotes are retained in `doc/evidence/stage2-presentation-boundaries-20261103/inputs/authorization.json`. The required revision03 scope/proof/result/router/view/terrain source, tests and contract are now applied for a new validation campaign. Optional shared-binary heap semantics remain unapplied. Prior failures and freezes01–04 remain immutable; large signed display is not yet accepted.
+
+Yan directs removal of the arbitrary scene-mark ceiling and neighbouring presentation limitations through the existing shared owners. The standing rule is **NO HARDCODED LIMITATION MUST BE INTRODUCED WITHOUT INFORMING ME !** This supersedes the earlier request for a new allocation decision merely to remove inherited presentation caps. Preserve explicit authored domain/resource allowances, structural/encoding correctness, authorization and deadlines; do not replace removed constants with other defaults or filtering. The current slice audits and removes scene/traversal/lens/label/coordinate/global asset ceilings and the generic text-field default, with separately labelled signed baselines and final source/evidence. Durable abolish and Stage 3 remain separate.
+
+**Partial implementation, not large-display acceptance:** the applied shared-owner correction is +68/−161 production lines (net −93): no scene/traversal/lens count ceilings, forced label shortening, arbitrary descriptor text/coordinate/global-resource maxima, or generic 4096-byte text default. Explicit authored allowances and real type/finite-rendering checks remain. Focused checks pass 53 Prolog/resource tests, 44 browser tests, one measured terrain-owner check and seven native adapter checks. These do not replace signed acceptance.
+
+The full signed fixture contains 287 predicates/574 marks before removal and 285/570 after retiring two obsolete helper predicates. `diagnostic-signed04` measured a 159,879-byte model invocation refused by the inherited 128 KiB nested-goal boundary (`protocol_error,request_binding`) and 858 catalogue enumerations. `reply-baseline01` separately records the 512 KiB result refusal. Automatic approval review rejected both proposed shared-boundary changes before execution. Unapplied patches, exact reasons, remaining caller constraints and the partial freeze are in the [presentation-limit handoff](../handoffs/stage2-d5-reconciliation-20261103.md#presentation-limit-removal-partial-freeze). Those prior rejections and proposals are historical; the renewed explicit authorization above now permits the applied required changes. No limit was raised, no subset substituted and no uncertain operation replayed. Full large signed contents, its denial/update/reconnect/unchanged-work campaign, and remaining transport-policy ownership stay open.
+
+## D6 ordinary predicate composition — architectural correction 2026-11-03
+
+Yan clarified that predicates are ordinary Prolog instances with class-owned Eidolons and ontology space contents. No predicate-specific client identity or domain dispatch is permitted. The earlier +41/−7 slice is behavioral evidence, not architectural completion. Refactor its special cases through ordinary entity references, authored workspace binding and the shared contents projection. This supersedes the earlier generic ancestry approval gate for display; entry, founding, physics and permission grants remain separate.
+
+## D6 predicate selection — accepted 2026-11-03
+
+**Confirmed direction:** Yan selected one object per predicate indicator (for example `likes/2`). Selecting it opens all clauses in the existing guarded source editor. Editing and reordering preserve the selected predicate and semantic clause order. Individually selectable clauses are deferred. This granularity approval did not settle deletion/recreation identity. Yan subsequently clarified ordinary class/instance/space display composition in the architectural correction above; that is the authority for the current display integration.
+
+**Current correction:** predicates use ordinary `entity_ref(Namespace,Genesis,predicate(Name/Arity))` subjects, class-local Prolog model/source recipes and the same recursive contents owner as other spaces. Authored `workspace(prolog_editor(Selection),...)` data binds the existing source editor. The predicate-specific client identity, selection event and dispatch have been removed. The earlier 37-check +41/−7 slice and freeze01–04 remain historical evidence; they do not describe current architecture. The current source, signed checks, failures and actual incremental delta are recorded in the [existing handoff](../handoffs/stage2-d5-reconciliation-20261103.md#ordinary-predicate-composition-correction).
+
+**Historical freeze01 boundaries (current removal and blockers are recorded above):** full large-ontology display was not accepted. The then-existing `quod:gui` scene policy in `gui_scene.pl` allowed 512 marks; the earlier observed 368 predicates require 736 marks with ordinary occurrence groups plus geometry. The full signed attempt ended in `proof_unavailable`, so the capacity calculation is not a claim that this was its only runtime cause. No filtering, pagination, budget increase or deadline change is introduced. `contents06` / `contents-signed07` passes 22 checks on a complete small ontology, including authored labels, three zero-work renewals, a real catalogue update, cycles and reconnect; it verifies the shared path only within its existing allowance. Yan subsequently authorized removal of those inherited presentation caps; this historical paragraph is not a new architecture approval gate. Definition-withdrawal remains blocked by the existing durable lifecycle: signed `abolish/1` becomes clause retractions and leaves an empty-but-defined catalogue entry. Empty definitions and deletion/recreation identity remain unchanged. D5 stays delivered; `moved-signed03` passes the affected shared-projection regression. The predicate/editor refactor passes `candidate-signed04` (48 checks), and delegated source denial passes `delegated-signed02` (14 checks). Stage 3/D7 and founding remain separate.
+

+%% A local source-draft transformation only. The existing guarded compiler and
+%% explicit Preview/Accept retain the original baseline, ACLs and outcome owner.
+%% These edits are raw authored facts; removal is never a cascade/domain delete.
+prolog_workbench_draft(Source, Change, Chunk) :-
+    term_variables([Source, Change], []),
+    '$quod_program_source'(Program, Source),
+    workbench_change(Change, Program, Next),
+    source_chunk_bytes(Bytes), '$quod_source_chunk'(terms(Next), Bytes, Chunk).
+%% The existing source-indicator primitive recognizes wire atom symbols without
+%% allocating them. A not-yet-authored class/name must remain data at this step.
+workbench_symbol(Symbol) :- nonvar(Symbol), '$quod_source_indicator'(Symbol, Symbol/0).
+workbench_fact(isa(C, P)) :- workbench_symbol(C), workbench_symbol(P).
+workbench_fact(instance_of(C, I)) :- workbench_symbol(C), term_variables(I, []).
+workbench_fact(attribute(I, K, V)) :- term_variables([I, K, V], []), workbench_symbol(K).
+workbench_change(add(Fact), Program, Next) :-
+    workbench_fact(Fact), Fact \= instance_of(_, _),
+    (workbench_member(Fact, Program) -> fail_with_reason(fact_already_present)
+    ; append(Program, [Fact], Next)).
+workbench_change(remove(Fact), Program, Next) :-
+    workbench_fact(Fact), workbench_remove(Program, Fact, Next).
+workbench_change(replace(Before, After), Program, Next) :-
+    workbench_fact(Before), workbench_fact(After),
+    functor(Before, Kind, Arity), functor(After, Kind, Arity),
+    After \= instance_of(_, _),
+    (workbench_same(Before, After) -> workbench_member(Before, Program), Next = Program
+    ; workbench_remove(Program, Before, Rest),
+      (workbench_member(After, Rest) -> fail_with_reason(fact_already_present)
+      ; append(Rest, [After], Next))).
+%% Ground wire symbols and their already-materialized atoms name the same fact.
+%% Reuse the source compiler's canonical identity; never allocate input atoms.
+workbench_same(A, B) :-
+    term_variables([A, B], []), prolog_clause(A, CA), prolog_clause(B, CB),
+    term_digest(CA, D), term_digest(CB, D).
+workbench_member(Fact, Program) :- member(Current, Program), workbench_same(Fact, Current), !.
+workbench_remove([Fact | Rest], Expected, Rest) :- workbench_same(Fact, Expected), !.
+workbench_remove([Fact | Rest], Expected, [Fact | Next]) :-
+    workbench_remove(Rest, Expected, Next).
+workbench_remove([], _, _) :- fail_with_reason(fact_not_present).

+    workbench_position(Index, X, Y),

+    (Kind == class -> Shape = <<"box">>, Colour = <<"#F9C80E">>,

+      Sizes = [f(<<"diameter">>, 500)], Ref = entity_ref(Ns, A, Depicted)),
+    Ref =.. [_ | RefParts], append(_, [Local], RefParts),
+    '$quod_program_source'([Local], Label),
+    Mark = mark(Id, Shape, Sizes, transform(X, Y, 0, 0, 0, 0),
+                surface(Colour, 0, 900, 0, []), label(Label, <<"above">>), depicts(Ns, A, Depicted)),
+    Next is Index + 1, workbench_marks(Rest, Ns, A, Next, Marks).
+workbench_links([], _, _, _, _, []).
+workbench_links([Fact | Rest], Nodes, Ns, A, Index, Marks) :-
+    (Fact = isa(C, P) -> Left = class_ref(Ns, A, C), Right = class_ref(Ns, A, P), Colour = <<"#F9C80E">>
+    ; Fact = instance_of(C, I), Left = entity_ref(Ns, A, I), Right = class_ref(Ns, A, C), Colour = <<"#6A90D9">>),
+    workbench_index(Nodes, Left, 0, L), workbench_index(Nodes, Right, 0, R),
+    workbench_position(L, X1, Y1), workbench_position(R, X2, Y2),
+    Width is abs(X2 - X1) + 20, Height is abs(Y2 - Y1) + 20,
+    X is (X1 + X2) // 2, Y is (Y1 + Y2) // 2, Z is -200,
+    mark_id(Index, Base), binary_codes(Base, Codes),
+    binary_codes(H, [104 | Codes]), binary_codes(V, [118 | Codes]),
+    Surface = surface(Colour, 0, 900, 0, []),
+    Horizontal = mark(H, <<"box">>, [f(<<"width">>, Width), f(<<"height">>, 20), f(<<"depth">>, 20)],
+                      transform(X, Y1, Z, 0, 0, 0), Surface, unlabelled, depicts_nothing),
+    Vertical = mark(V, <<"box">>, [f(<<"width">>, 20), f(<<"height">>, Height), f(<<"depth">>, 20)],
+                    transform(X2, Y, Z, 0, 0, 0), Surface, unlabelled, depicts_nothing),
+    Next is Index + 1, workbench_links(Rest, Nodes, Ns, A, Next, More),
+    Marks = [Horizontal, Vertical | More].
+workbench_index([node(_, Ref) | _], Ref, Index, Index) :- !.
+workbench_index([_ | Rest], Ref, Index, Result) :-
+    Next is Index + 1, workbench_index(Rest, Ref, Next, Result).
+%% Presentation spacing is independent of source identity and label length.
+workbench_position(Index, X, Y) :-
+    X is (Index mod 6) * 3000, Y is -(Index // 6) * 1200.

+admit_answer(#est{} = St, Answer) ->

+admit_answer(Handle, Answer) ->
+    State = get_session(Handle),

+    end.

+    end.

+self_scoped_request_materializes_and_charges_callable_test() ->
+    {PublicKey, _} = quod_identity:generate(),
+    Ns = <<"request:self">>,
+    {ok, #{blob := AgentRef}} = quod_agent_ref:from_text(
+        Ns, <<71:256>>, <<"person(self_symbol).">>, 2),
+    {ok, #{reference := {agent_instance_ref, Ns, _, {person, self_symbol}}}} =
+        quod_agent_ref:decode(AgentRef),
+    Limit = #{window_ms => 60000, max_total => 8,
+              max_per_key => 1, max_keys => 8},
+    Head = unique_symbol(<<"self_scoped_head_">>),
+    Next = unique_symbol(<<"self_scoped_head_">>),
+    Data = {'$quod_symbol', <<"self_scoped_data">>},
+    Goal = fun(Name) ->
+        {'::', Ns, {assertz, {{'$quod_symbol', <<":-">>},
+                             {{'$quod_symbol', Name}, Data}, true}}}
+    end,
+    with_auth(#{max_materialized_atoms => 1024,
+                symbol_signing_key_limit => Limit,
+                symbol_peer_limit => Limit}, fun() ->
+        {ok, _, {'::', Ns, {assertz, {':-', {Callable, Data}, true}}}} =
+            quod_client_auth:materialize_request(PublicKey, ?PEER, AgentRef, Goal(Head)),
+        ?assert(is_atom(Callable)),
+        ?assertEqual(Head, atom_to_binary(Callable, utf8)),
+        ?assertEqual({error, client_goal_rate_limited},
+            quod_client_auth:materialize_request(PublicKey, ?PEER, AgentRef, Goal(Next))),
+        ?assertError(badarg, binary_to_existing_atom(Next, utf8))
+    end).

+remote_large_result_preserves_exact_correlation_test() ->
+    {ok, Payload} = quod_wire_term:encode_bindings(
+        #{<<"X">> => binary:copy(<<$x>>, (1024 * 1024))}, unlimited),
+    Ref = {transaction, <<"quod:test">>, <<1:256>>, <<2:256>>},
+    lists:foreach(fun({Cursor, Result}) ->
+        with_router(fun(Router, Link, Fixture) ->
+            Caller = submit_async(Router, Fixture, Cursor, 1000),
+            {Request, _} = sent_request(Link),
+            ok = quod_client_goal_router:test_send_result(Link, Request, Result),
+            Frame = target_frame(Link),
+            {ok, Response} = quod_client_goal_endpoint:decode_response(Frame),
+            Id = quod_client_goal_endpoint:request_id(Request),
+            {ok, Encoded} = quod_client_result:encode(Result),
+            Expected = case Cursor of none -> {result, Id, Encoded};
+                _ -> {cursor_result, Id, Cursor, Encoded} end,
+            ?assertEqual(Expected, Response),
+            Router ! {quod_message, {?PEER, Link}, quod_client_goal_endpoint:channel(), Frame},
+            receive {Caller, {ok, _, {normalized, Result}}} -> ok
+            after 1000 -> error(large_result_missing) end,
+            case Cursor of
+                none -> ok;
+                _ ->
+                    await_stats(Router, #{correlations => 0, routes => 1}),
+                    Stopper = cursor_async(Router, Cursor, stop, 1000),
+                    {{cursor, StopId, Cursor, stop}, _} = sent_request(Link),
+                    {ok, Stopped} = quod_client_result:encode(stopped),
+                    respond(Router, Link, {cursor_result, StopId, Cursor, Stopped}),
+                    receive {Stopper, {ok, _, {normalized, stopped}}} -> ok
+                    after 1000 -> error(cursor_stop_result_missing) end
+            end,
+            await_stats(Router, #{correlations => 0, routes => 0}),
+            assert_released_lease(Link, Router)
+        end)
+    end, [{none, {answers, 7, [Payload]}},
+          {<<9:256>>, {solution, <<9:256>>, 7, Payload}},
+          {none, {committed, [Payload], Ref}}]).
+
+target_encoding_keeps_outcome_and_distinguishes_invalid_from_size_test() ->
+    Parent = self(), Link = spawn(fun() -> link_loop(Parent) end),
+    Ref = {transaction, <<"quod:test">>, <<1:256>>, <<2:256>>},
+    Request = {submit, <<8:128>>, <<>>, <<0:512>>, none, []},
+    try
+        lists:foreach(fun({Result, Expected}) ->
+            ok = quod_client_goal_router:test_send_result(Link, Request, Result),
+            {ok, {result, <<8:128>>, Blob}} = quod_client_goal_endpoint:decode_response(target_frame(Link)),
+            ?assertEqual({ok, Expected}, quod_client_result:decode(Blob))
+        end, [{{pending, Ref}, {pending, Ref}},
+              {{committed, [], Ref}, {committed, [], Ref}},

+              {{error, {material_limit, proof, answer_bytes, 10, 9}},
+               {error, {material_limit, proof, answer_bytes, 10, 9}}}]),
+        ok = quod_client_goal_router:test_send_response(Link, {error, <<8:128>>, invalid_signature}),
+        ?assertEqual({ok, {error, <<8:128>>, invalid_signature}},
+                     quod_client_goal_endpoint:decode_response(target_frame(Link)))
+    after Link ! stop end.

+late_large_reply_cannot_restore_cancelled_correlation_test() ->
+    with_router(fun(Router, Link, Fixture) ->
+        Caller = submit_async(Router, Fixture, none, 1000),
+        {Request, _} = sent_request(Link),
+        Monitor = monitor(process, Caller), exit(Caller, kill),
+        receive {'DOWN', Monitor, process, Caller, killed} -> ok
+        after 1000 -> error(caller_not_cancelled) end,
+        await_stats(Router, #{correlations => 0, routes => 0}),
+        {ok, Payload} = quod_wire_term:encode_bindings(
+            #{<<"X">> => binary:copy(<<$x>>, (1024 * 1024))}, unlimited),
+        ok = quod_client_goal_router:test_send_result(Link, Request, {answers, 7, [Payload]}),
+        Router ! {quod_message, {?PEER, Link}, quod_client_goal_endpoint:channel(), target_frame(Link)},
+        ?assertMatch(#{correlations := 0, routes := 0}, quod_client_goal_router:test_stats(Router))
+    end).
+
+target_frame(Link) ->
+    receive {link_frame, Link, Frame} -> Frame
+    after 1000 -> error(target_response_missing) end.

+unrepresentable_iodata_refuses_before_allocation_or_enqueue_test() ->
+    %% Reuse one binary: the declared 4 GiB length is measured without making
+    %% a 4 GiB allocation, starting a link, or changing transport authority.
+    Payload = lists:duplicate(4096, binary:copy(<<0>>, 1024 * 1024)),
+    ?assertEqual({error, frame_unrepresentable}, quod_link:send(self(), Payload)),
+    ?assertEqual({error, frame_unrepresentable}, quod_link:send_ordered(self(), Payload)),
+    ?assertEqual({error, frame_unrepresentable}, quod_link:send_reliable(self(), Payload, 1000)),
+    receive {send, _} -> error(unrepresentable_frame_enqueued);
+            {send_ordered, _} -> error(unrepresentable_frame_enqueued);
+            {send_reliable, _, _, _, _} -> error(unrepresentable_frame_enqueued)
+    after 0 -> ok end.

+
+binary_heavy_nested_scope_preserves_authorization_and_cancellation_test() ->
+    lists:foreach(fun(Allowed) ->
+        Facts = case Allowed of true -> [{can_invoke,{'_'},{'_'},{'_'},{'_'}}]; false -> [] end,
+        {Handle,MRef} = quod_scope_session:start(id(95),key(96),self(),
+            <<"quod:large-nested">>,key(97),0,committed(Facts),self(),
+            #{principal => {node,key(98)},request_binding => none,
+              deadline_ms => quod_time:mono_ms()+5000}),
+        {quod_scope_session,Worker,_,ProofId,SessionRef,_,_} = Handle,
+        Invocation = id(99), Value = binary:copy(<<$v>>,160000),
+        try
+            {ok,Opened} = quod_scope_session:invoke_open(Handle,Invocation,{'=',Value,Value},
+                [{<<"quod:origin">>,key(100)}],quod_transaction_scope:empty_selection()),
+            ?assertEqual({opened,Invocation},receive_scope_reply(Worker,ProofId,SessionRef,Opened)),
+            {ok,Next} = quod_scope_session:invoke_next(Handle,Invocation,1),
+            Reply = receive_scope_reply(Worker,ProofId,SessionRef,Next),
+            case Allowed of
+                true -> ?assertMatch({solution,1,_,_,false},Reply);
+                false -> ?assertMatch({complete,1,[{not_allowed,<<"quod:large-nested">>}|_],false},Reply)
+            end
+        after
+            ok = quod_scope_session:close(Handle),
+            receive {'DOWN',MRef,process,Worker,_} -> ok
+            after 1000 -> error(large_scope_not_retired) end
+        end
+    end,[true,false]).

+    {ok, {'::', _, {Callable, _}}} =
+        quod_wire_term:materialize_goal_symbols(Inner, <<"wire:owner">>),
+    ?assert(is_atom(Callable)).
